  • Enums not in OpenBCM

Enums not in OpenBCM

Enums and enum values that the Cint interpreter on this build registers but that OpenBCM 6.5.27’s Cint does not. Values are the numbers this build uses; write the names, not the numbers, in programs. Back to Cint API on this build.

New enums

bcm_capability_* (3)

    bcmCapabilityArrayModStatefulEventFormatsEgr = 5,
